            liveBody.scrollLeft += deltaX;
          });
        }, { passive:false });
      }
      // 기타/OOC는 크게 편집 대신 클릭했을 때 짧은 내용을 가능한 한 한 번에 볼 수 있게 아래로 펼칩니다.
      if (slot.group === 'extra') {
        const compactHeight = editorHeightPreference(slot);
        const fitExtraHeight = () => {
          if (isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
          ta.style.height = 'auto';
          ta.style.height = `${Math.max(compactHeight, Math.min(620, ta.scrollHeight + 6))}px`;
        };
        ta.addEventListener('focus', fitExtraHeight);
        ta.addEventListener('input', () => { if (document.activeElement === ta) fitExtraHeight(); });
        ta.addEventListener('blur', () => { ta.style.height = `${compactHeight}px`; });
      }
      if (editorCopy) editorCopy.onclick = async () => {
        const ok = await copyPlainText(ta.value);
        notify(ok ? `‘${slot.title}’ 내용을 복사했습니다.` : '내용 복사에 실패했습니다.', ok ? 'success' : 'error', 3000);
      };
      if (editorSelect) editorSelect.onclick = () => { ta.focus(); ta.select(); };
      if (editorClean) editorClean.onclick = () => {
        if (cleanupUndo && ta.value === cleanupUndo.cleaned) {
          ta.value = cleanupUndo.original;
          cleanupUndo = null;
          clearTimeout(cleanupUndoTimer);
          editorClean.textContent = '붙여넣기 정리';
          commitTextareaValue();
          notify('정리 전 내용으로 되돌렸습니다.', 'success', 3000);
          return;
        }
        const cleaned = cleanedPastedText(ta.value);
        if (cleaned === ta.value) { notify('정리할 공백이나 코드블록 표식이 없습니다.', 'success', 2600); return; }
        cleanupUndo = { original:ta.value, cleaned };
        ta.value = cleaned;
        editorClean.textContent = '정리 되돌리기';
        commitTextareaValue();
        clearTimeout(cleanupUndoTimer);
        cleanupUndoTimer = setTimeout(() => { cleanupUndo = null; if (editorClean) editorClean.textContent = '붙여넣기 정리'; }, 12000);
        notify('붙여넣기 형식을 정리했습니다. 12초 동안 버튼으로 되돌릴 수 있습니다.', 'success', 4200);
      };
      if (itemTemplateButton) itemTemplateButton.onclick = () => {
        if (!isUntouchedImportantItemTemplate(ta.value) && !confirm('현재 내용을 빈 중요 물품 작성 틀로 교체할까요?\n작성한 내용은 사라집니다.')) return;
        ta.value = IMPORTANT_ITEM_CONTENT_TEMPLATE;
        commitTextareaValue();
        refreshItemTemplateExample();
        ta.focus();
        const firstValuePosition = ta.value.indexOf('=') + 1;
        ta.setSelectionRange(firstValuePosition, firstValuePosition);
        notify('작성 틀을 넣었습니다. = 뒤에 내용을 채워주세요.', 'success', 3200);
      };
      if (editorFocus) editorFocus.onclick = () => openDetachedEditor(slot, ta);
      if (guideVariantSelect) guideVariantSelect.value = activeGuideVariant;
      if (guideTextarea) guideTextarea.value = getGuideText(slot.id, activeGuideVariant);
      const refreshGuideModifiedState = (value = guideTextarea?.value) => {
        if (!guideToggle) return false;
        const modified = isGuideTextModified(slot.id, activeGuideVariant, value);
        guideToggle.classList.toggle('is-modified', modified);
        guideToggle.title = modified ? '기본값에서 수정된 지침' : '기본 지침';
        return modified;
      };
      refreshGuideModifiedState();
      if (guideToggle && guidePanel) {
        guideToggle.onclick = (e) => {
          e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
          d.open = true;
          const willOpen = guidePanel.hidden;
          guidePanel.hidden = !willOpen;
          guideToggle.classList.toggle('is-open', willOpen);
          if (willOpen) setTimeout(() => guideTextarea?.focus(), 0);
        };
      }
      if (guideTextarea) guideTextarea.oninput = () => {
        refreshGuideModifiedState(guideTextarea.value);
        clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer);
        guideSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => saveGuideText(slot.id, guideTextarea.value, activeGuideVariant), 250);
      };
      if (guideVariantSelect) guideVariantSelect.onchange = (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        clearTimeout(guideSaveTimer);
        if (guideTextarea) saveGuideText(slot.id, guideTextarea.value, activeGuideVariant);
        activeGuideVariant = saveLogSummaryGuideVariant(guideVariantSelect.value, false);
        guideVariantSelect.value = activeGuideVariant;
        if (guideTextarea) guideTextarea.value = getGuideText(slot.id, activeGuideVariant);
        refreshGuideModifiedState();
      };
      if (guideCopy) guideCopy.onclick = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
        if (guideTextarea) saveGuideText(slot.id, guideTextarea.value, activeGuideVariant);
        const ok = await copyPlainText(externalChatTitleInstruction(slot.id)+'\n\n'+(guideTextarea?.value || ''));
        notify(ok ? `${slot.id === 'logSummary' ? `${logSummaryGuideVariantLabel(activeGuideVariant)} ` : ''}지침을 클립보드에 복사했습니다.` : '지침 복사에 실패했습니다.', ok ? 'success' : 'error', 3200);
      };
      if (guideReset) guideReset.onclick = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
        const guideLabel = slot.id === 'logSummary' ? `날짜요약 ${logSummaryGuideVariantLabel(activeGuideVariant)}` : '현재상태';
        if (!confirm(`${guideLabel} 지침을 기본값으로 복원할까요?\n직접 수정한 내용은 사라집니다.`)) return;
        const restored = resetGuideText(slot.id, activeGuideVariant);
        if (guideTextarea) guideTextarea.value = restored;
        refreshGuideModifiedState(restored);
        notify(`${guideLabel} 지침을 기본값으로 복원했습니다.`, 'success', 3200);
      };
      const refreshAutoTerms = () => {
        if (!autoTermsEl) return;
        const terms = slot.group === 'item' ? importantItemAutomaticTerms(slot) : characterAutomaticTerms(slot);
        const label = slot.group === 'item' ? '물품 자동 감지어' : '자동 선택 감지어';
        const empty = slot.group === 'item' ? '물품 이름을 입력하면 자동 생성됩니다.' : '캐릭터 이름을 입력하면 자동 생성됩니다.';
        autoTermsEl.innerHTML = `<strong>${label}</strong> · ${esc(terms.slice(0, 10).join(' · ') || empty)}${terms.length > 10 ? ' · …' : ''}`;
      };

      cb.onclick = e => e.stopPropagation();
      cb.onchange = async () => {
        // debounce 저장 전에 바로 체크한 경우에도 현재 화면의 최신 입력값으로 carrier를 재구성합니다.
        slot.content = ta.value;
        if (titleInput) slot.title = titleInput.value.trim() || fallbackTitle;
        if (retentionInput) slot.retentionTurns = normalizeRetentionTurns(retentionInput.value);
        const desired = cb.checked;
        const previousManualOverride = slot.manualInjectionOverride;
        if (slot.group === 'character' || slot.group === 'item') slot.manualInjectionOverride = desired ? 'on' : 'off';
        if (!room.pending) { slot.enabled = desired; await saveRoom(room); refreshStatsOnly(); return; }
        const question = desired
          ? `현재 컨텍스트가 주입 중입니다.\n‘${slot.title}’ 항목을 현재 주입에 추가할까요?\n확인하면 이 항목의 ${retentionLabel(slot.retentionTurns)} 유지 주기가 지금부터 새로 시작됩니다.`
          : `현재 컨텍스트가 주입 중입니다.\n‘${slot.title}’ 항목을 현재 주입에서 제거할까요?`;
        if (!confirm(question)) { cb.checked = !desired; slot.enabled = !desired; slot.manualInjectionOverride = previousManualOverride; refreshStatsOnly(); return; }
        cb.disabled = true;
        try { await setSlotEnabledDuringPending(room, slot, desired); }
        catch (e) { cb.checked = !desired; slot.manualInjectionOverride = previousManualOverride; notify(`주입 변경 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500); }
        finally { cb.disabled = false; renderModalIfOpen(); }
      };
      if (retentionInput) retentionInput.onchange = async () => {
        slot.retentionTurns = normalizeRetentionTurns(retentionInput.value);
        if (room.pending) {
          const targets = slot.id === 'logSummary'
            ? (room.pending.items || []).filter(i => i.slotId === 'logSummary' || i.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || i.group === 'log-auto')
            : (room.pending.items || []).filter(i => i.slotId === slot.id);
          if (targets.length) {
            for (const item of targets) {
              item.totalTurns = slot.retentionTurns;
              item.usedTurns = Number(item.totalTurns || 0) === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(Number(item.usedTurns || 0), Math.max(0, Number(item.totalTurns || 0) - 1));
            }
            await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'retention-change').catch(e => notify(`유지주기 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000));
          }
        }
        await saveRoom(room);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      };
      const aliasInput = d.querySelector('.rpcm-alias-input');
      if (itemFolderInput) {
        itemFolderInput.oninput = () => { slot.itemFolder = itemFolderInput.value.trim(); queueRoomAutoSave(room); };
        itemFolderInput.onchange = () => { slot.itemFolder = itemFolderInput.value.trim(); moveSlotFolderInPlace(d,slot); queueRoomAutoSave(room); };
      }
      if (extraCategoryInput) {
        extraCategoryInput.oninput = () => { slot.extraCategory = extraCategoryInput.value.trim(); queueRoomAutoSave(room); };
        extraCategoryInput.onchange = () => {slot.extraCategory=extraCategoryInput.value.trim();moveSlotFolderInPlace(d,slot);queueRoomAutoSave(room);};
      }
      if (aliasInput) aliasInput.oninput = debounce(() => { slot.aliases = aliasInput.value.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean); queueRoomAutoSave(room); }, 250);
      const pinnedInput = d.querySelector('.rpcm-auto-pinned');
      if (pinnedInput) pinnedInput.onchange = async () => {
        const desired = pinnedInput.checked;
        const previous = { pinned:!!slot.autoPinned, excluded:!!slot.autoExcluded, enabled:!!slot.enabled, lastAutoMatch:slot.lastAutoMatch };
        if (desired && room.pending && !confirm(`‘${slot.title}’을 항상 주입 선택하고 현재 주입에도 즉시 추가할까요?`)) {
          pinnedInput.checked = previous.pinned;
          return;
        }
        slot.autoPinned = desired;
        try {
          if (desired) {
            slot.autoExcluded = false;
            if (excludedInput) excludedInput.checked = false;
            slot.lastAutoMatch = '사용자 고정';
            if (room.pending) await setSlotEnabledDuringPending(room, slot, true);
            else slot.enabled = true;
            cb.checked = true;
          }
          await saveRoom(room);
          refreshStatsOnly();
        } catch (e) {
          slot.autoPinned = previous.pinned;
          slot.autoExcluded = previous.excluded;
          slot.enabled = previous.enabled;
          slot.lastAutoMatch = previous.lastAutoMatch;
          pinnedInput.checked = previous.pinned;
          if (excludedInput) excludedInput.checked = previous.excluded;
          cb.checked = previous.enabled;
          try { await saveRoom(room); } catch (_) {}
          refreshStatsOnly();
          notify(`고정 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000);
        }
      };
      const excludedInput = d.querySelector('.rpcm-auto-excluded');
      if (excludedInput) excludedInput.onchange = async () => {
        slot.autoExcluded = excludedInput.checked;
        if (slot.autoExcluded && slot.autoPinned) { slot.autoPinned = false; if (pinnedInput) pinnedInput.checked = false; }
        await saveRoom(room);
        refreshStatsOnly();
      };
      if (titleInput) {
        // 기본 이름은 클릭 즉시 비워 새 이름을 바로 입력할 수 있게 합니다.
        // 아무것도 입력하지 않고 빠져나오면 그룹의 기본 이름으로 되돌립니다.
        if (slot.group === 'extra' || slot.group === 'item') {
          const defaultPattern = slot.group === 'item' ? /^중요 물품(?:\s+\d+)?$/ : /^기타(?:\s+\d+)?$/;
          const restoredTitle = slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : '기타';
          titleInput.addEventListener('focus', () => {
            if (defaultPattern.test(titleInput.value.trim())) titleInput.value = '';
          });
          titleInput.addEventListener('blur', () => {
            if (titleInput.value.trim()) return;
            titleInput.value = restoredTitle;
            slot.title = restoredTitle;
            name.textContent = `${slot.group === 'item' ? '📦 ' : ''}${restoredTitle}`;
            refreshAutoTerms();
            queueRoomAutoSave(room);
          });
        }
        titleInput.oninput = () => {
          markManualSlotEdit(room,slot);
          slot.title = titleInput.value;
          name.textContent = `${slot.group === 'item' ? '📦 ' : ''}${slot.group === 'item' ? importantItemHeaderParts(titleInput.value || fallbackTitle).name : titleInput.value || fallbackTitle}`;
          refreshStatsOnly();
          refreshAutoTerms();
          queueRoomAutoSave(room);
        };
        if (slot.group === 'item') titleInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
          slot.title = titleInput.value.trim() || '중요 물품';
          const duplicates = duplicateImportantItemGroups(room);
          if (!duplicates.some(group => group.items.some(item => String(item.id) === String(slot.id)))) return;
          notify(`‘${slot.title}’과 같은 물품이 있습니다. 상단 경고에서 둘 다 유지하거나 하나만 남길 수 있습니다.`, 'warn', 5200);
          renderModalIfOpen();
        });
      }
      const mobileEditing = isMobileManagerLayout();
      const refreshSlotStatsSoon = debounce(() => {
        refreshStatsOnly();
        refreshAutoTerms();
      }, mobileEditing ? 450 : 100);
      const syncPendingContentEdit = debounce(async () => {
        if (!room.pending) return;
        if (rpcmHasActiveComposition()) { syncPendingContentEdit(); return; }
        try {
          // 주입 중 본문이 바뀌면 현재 carrier의 복사본도 갱신합니다.
          // 로그요약은 최신/관련/직접/고정 날짜 후보를 다시 계산합니다.
          await syncEditedSlotIntoPending(room, slot, slot.id === 'logSummary' ? 'log-content-edit' : 'slot-content-edit');
        } catch (e) {
          notify(`현재 주입 내용 갱신 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500);
        }
      // 대용량 붙여넣기 직후 이어지는 입력을 한 번으로 묶어 불필요한 PATCH를 줄입니다.
      }, slot.id === 'logSummary' ? (mobileEditing ? 2800 : 1800) : (mobileEditing ? 1800 : 700));
      ta.oninput = () => {
        markManualSlotEdit(room,slot);
        slot.content = ta.value;
        if (readPreview) paintReadView();
        refreshItemTemplateExample();
        count.textContent = `${formatCount(ta.value.length)}자`;
        const itemEditCount = d.querySelector('.rpcm-item-edit-count');
        if (itemEditCount) itemEditCount.textContent = `${formatCount(ta.value.length)}자`;
        const mobileEditCount = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-mobile-edit-count');
        if (ta.classList.contains('rpcm-mobile-active-editor') && mobileEditCount) mobileEditCount.textContent = `${formatCount(ta.value.length)}자`;
        refreshSlotStatsSoon();
        queueRoomAutoSave(room, mobileEditing ? 900 : 500);
        if (room.pending?.items?.some(item => item.slotId === slot.id || (slot.id === 'logSummary' && (item.sourceSlotId === 'logSummary' || item.group === 'log-auto')))) setCarrierSyncStatusUi(room, 'queued');
        syncPendingContentEdit();
      };

      const del = d.querySelector('.rpcm-delete-btn');
      if (del) {
        del.onclick = async e => {
          e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
          const title = slot.title || '이 항목';
          const activeInPending = !!room.pending?.items?.some(i => i.slotId === slot.id && (Number(i.totalTurns || 0) === 0 || Number(i.usedTurns || 0) < Number(i.totalTurns || 0)));
          if (!confirm(activeInPending ? `'${title}' 항목은 현재 주입에도 들어 있습니다.\n현재 주입에서 제거하고 항목도 삭제할까요?` : `'${title}' 항목을 삭제할까요?`)) return;
          const previousSlots = room.slots.slice();
          const previousPending = room.pending?.items?.slice();
          let synced = false;
          try {
            room.slots = room.slots.filter(x => x.id !== slot.id);
            if (activeInPending) {
              room.pending.items = room.pending.items.filter(i => i.slotId !== slot.id);
              await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'delete-slot');
            }
            synced = true;
            if (slot.group === 'extra' && !room.slots.some(x => x.group === 'extra')) room.slots.push(makeDynamicSlot('extra', '기타'));
            await saveRoom(room);
            renderModalIfOpen();
          } catch (error) {
            if (!synced) {
              room.slots = previousSlots;
              if (previousPending && room.pending) room.pending.items = previousPending;
            }
            notify(`항목 삭제 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6500);
          }
        };
      }
      return d;
    }

    const fixed = room.slots.filter(s => s.group === 'fixed');
    const chars = room.slots.filter(s => s.group === 'character');
    const items = room.slots.filter(s => s.group === 'item');
    function updateItemDeleteSelection() {
      const selected = overlay.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-section-item .rpcm-item-delete-select:checked').length;
      const indicator = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-item-selected-count');
      if (indicator) indicator.textContent = `${selected}개 선택`;
      const button = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-items-delete-selected');
      if (button) { button.disabled = selected === 0; button.textContent = selected ? `선택 삭제 (${selected})` : '선택 삭제'; }
    }
    const extras = room.slots.filter(s => s.group === 'extra');
    fixed.forEach(slot => {
      if (slot.id === 'currentState') currentStateWrap?.appendChild(createSlotCard(slot, true));
      else if (slot.id === 'logSummary') logSummaryWrap?.appendChild(createSlotCard(slot, true));
    });
    if (!chars.length) charWrap.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-empty">아직 캐릭터 설정이 없습니다. 위의 ‘＋ 캐릭터 추가’를 눌러 인물별로 등록하세요.</div>`;
    else chars.forEach(slot => charWrap.appendChild(createSlotCard(slot, false)));
    if (state.itemFolderFilterRoomId !== String(room.chatId)) { state.itemFolderFilterRoomId = String(room.chatId); state.itemFolderFilter = 'all'; }
    const itemActors = personCoreViewModel(room).actors;
    const folderActor = folder => itemActors.find(actor => actor.name === folder)
      || itemActors.find(actor => (actor.aliases || []).includes(folder)) || null;
    const itemFolderOf = slot => String(slot.itemFolder || '').trim();
    const countFor = filter => items.filter(slot => {
      const folder = itemFolderOf(slot);
      return filter === 'all' || (filter === 'unclassified' && !folder)
        || (filter === 'other' && !!folder && !folderActor(folder))
        || (filter.startsWith('person:') && folderActor(folder)?.name === filter.slice(7));
    }).length;
    const filters = [['all','전체'],['unclassified','미분류'],...itemActors.map(actor => [`person:${actor.name}`,actor.name]),['other','기타']];
    if(!filters.some(([key])=>key===state.itemFolderFilter))state.itemFolderFilter='all';
    const hiddenPeople=new Set(Array.isArray(room.hiddenItemFilterPeople)?room.hiddenItemFilterPeople:[]);
    if(state.itemFolderFilter.startsWith('person:')&&hiddenPeople.has(state.itemFolderFilter.slice(7)))state.itemFolderFilter='all';
    itemWrap.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', `<nav class="rpcm-item-person-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="중요 물품 인물 필터">${filters.map(([key,label])=>{
      const tab=`<button type="button" role="tab" data-item-filter="${esc(key)}" aria-selected="${state.itemFolderFilter===key}" class="${state.itemFolderFilter===key?'is-selected':''}">${esc(label)} <small>${countFor(key)}</small></button>`;
      return key.startsWith('person:')?`<span class="rpcm-item-person-filter" data-item-person-wrapper="${esc(label)}" ${hiddenPeople.has(label)?'hidden':''}>${tab}<button type="button" class="rpcm-item-filter-eye" data-item-person-hide="${esc(label)}" title="${esc(label)} 필터 숨기기" aria-label="${esc(label)} 필터 숨기기">${rpcmItemFilterEye()}</button></span>`:tab;
    }).join('')}<button type="button" class="rpcm-item-filter-manager" data-item-filter-manager aria-label="숨긴 인물 필터 관리" title="인물 필터 표시 관리">${rpcmItemFilterEye(hiddenPeople.size>0)}<span>인물 표시${hiddenPeople.size?` · 숨김 ${hiddenPeople.size}`:''}</span></button></nav>`);
    const personTabs=itemWrap.previousElementSibling;
    const applyPersonVisibility=async name=>{
      const previous=[...(room.hiddenItemFilterPeople||[])],hidden=new Set(previous);
      if(hidden.has(name))hidden.delete(name);else hidden.add(name);
      room.hiddenItemFilterPeople=[...hidden];
      try{await saveRoom(room);}catch(error){room.hiddenItemFilterPeople=previous;notify('인물 필터 표시 저장 실패: '+error.message,'error');throw error;}
      personTabs.querySelectorAll('[data-item-person-wrapper]').forEach(node=>node.hidden=hidden.has(node.dataset.itemPersonWrapper));
      if(state.itemFolderFilter===`person:${name}`&&hidden.has(name))personTabs.querySelector('[data-item-filter="all"]')?.click();
      const manager=personTabs.querySelector('[data-item-filter-manager]');manager.innerHTML=`${rpcmItemFilterEye(hidden.size>0)}<span>인물 표시${hidden.size?` · 숨김 ${hidden.size}`:''}</span>`;
    };
    personTabs?.addEventListener('click',async event=>{
      const eye=event.target.closest('[data-item-person-hide]');if(eye){event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();eye.disabled=true;try{await applyPersonVisibility(eye.dataset.itemPersonHide);}catch{}finally{eye.disabled=false;}return;}
      if(event.target.closest('[data-item-filter-manager]')){openItemFilterVisibility(room,itemActors,applyPersonVisibility);return;}
      const tab=event.target.closest('[data-item-filter]');if(!tab)return;
      state.itemFolderFilter=tab.dataset.itemFilter;
      personTabs.querySelectorAll('[data-item-filter]').forEach(node=>{const active=node===tab;node.classList.toggle('is-selected',active);node.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(active));});
      itemWrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-item-folder').forEach(node=>{const folder=node.dataset.itemFolder;node.hidden=!(state.itemFolderFilter==='all'||state.itemFolderFilter==='unclassified'&&!folder||state.itemFolderFilter==='other'&&!!folder&&!folderActor(folder)||state.itemFolderFilter.startsWith('person:')&&folderActor(folder)?.name===state.itemFolderFilter.slice(7));});
    });
    if (!items.length) itemWrap.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-empty">아직 중요 물품이 없습니다. ‘목록 한 번에 붙여넣기’ 또는 ‘＋ 중요 물품 추가’로 등록하세요.</div>`;
    else {
      const folderGroups = new Map();
      for (const slot of items) {
        const folder = itemFolderOf(slot);
        if (!folderGroups.has(folder)) folderGroups.set(folder, []);
        folderGroups.get(folder).push(slot);
      }
      const orderedFolders = [...folderGroups].sort(([a],[b]) => {
        const rank = name => !name ? -1 : folderActor(name) ? itemActors.indexOf(folderActor(name)) : itemActors.length;
        return rank(a) - rank(b);
      });
      for (const [folder, folderItems] of orderedFolders) {
        const section = document.createElement('details');
        section.className = 'rpcm-item-folder';
        section.dataset.itemFolder = folder;
        section.hidden = !(state.itemFolderFilter === 'all' || (state.itemFolderFilter === 'unclassified' && !folder) || (state.itemFolderFilter === 'other' && !!folder && !folderActor(folder)) || (state.itemFolderFilter.startsWith('person:') && folderActor(folder)?.name === state.itemFolderFilter.slice(7)));
        section.open = !hadItemFolders || previouslyOpenItemFolders.has(folder);
        section.innerHTML = `<summary><strong>${!folder ? '🗂️' : '📁'} ${esc(folder || '미분류')}${folder === '미분류' ? ' (사용자 폴더)' : ''}</strong><span>${folderItems.length}개</span></summary><div class="rpcm-item-folder-body"></div>`;
        const body = section.querySelector('.rpcm-item-folder-body');
        folderItems.forEach(slot => body.appendChild(createSlotCard(slot, false)));
        itemWrap.appendChild(section);
      }
    }
    itemWrap.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="rpcm-unclassified-drop" data-drop-unclassified="item">이곳에 놓으면 미분류로 이동</div>');
    if (!extras.length) extraWrap.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-empty">기타 항목이 없습니다. 위에서 항목이나 카테고리를 추가하세요.</div>';
    else {
      const categories = new Map();
      for (const slot of extras) {
        const category = String(slot.extraCategory || '').trim() || '미분류';
        if (!categories.has(category)) categories.set(category, []);
        categories.get(category).push(slot);
      }
      for (const [category, members] of categories) {
        const folder = document.createElement('details');
        folder.className = 'rpcm-extra-folder';
        folder.dataset.extraCategory = category;
        folder.open = !hadExtraFolders || previouslyOpenExtraCategories.has(category);
        folder.innerHTML = `<summary><strong>${category === '미분류' ? '🗂️' : '📁'} ${esc(category)}</strong><span>${members.length}개</span></summary><div class="rpcm-extra-folder-body"></div>`;
        members.forEach(slot => folder.querySelector('.rpcm-extra-folder-body').appendChild(createSlotCard(slot, false)));
        extraWrap.appendChild(folder);
      }
    }
    extraWrap.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="rpcm-unclassified-drop" data-drop-unclassified="extra">이곳에 놓으면 미분류로 이동</div>');
    charWrap.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="rpcm-unclassified-drop" data-drop-unclassified="character">이곳에 놓으면 목록 맨 뒤로 이동</div>');

    // 목록의 ID와 원문은 그대로 두고, 저장 순서와 폴더 이름만 이동합니다.
    const moveListedSlot = async (group, dragId, target) => {
      const source = room.slots.find(slot => String(slot.id) === dragId && slot.group === group);
      if (!source || !target) return;
      const card = target.closest('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]');
      if (card?.dataset.slotId === dragId) return;
      const folderElement = target.closest('.rpcm-item-folder, .rpcm-extra-folder');
      const destination = group === 'item' ? folderElement?.dataset.itemFolder : group === 'extra' ? folderElement?.dataset.extraCategory : '';
      const folder = target.matches('.rpcm-unclassified-drop') ? (group === 'item' ? '' : '미분류') : destination;
      if (group !== 'character' && folder === undefined) return;
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const beforeId = card?.dataset.slotId;
      const oldIndex = room.slots.findIndex(slot => String(slot.id) === dragId);
      if (oldIndex < 0) return;
      const [moved] = room.slots.splice(oldIndex, 1);
      if (group === 'item') moved.itemFolder = folder;
      if (group === 'extra') moved.extraCategory = folder === '미분류' ? '' : folder;
      let insertAt = beforeId ? room.slots.findIndex(slot => String(slot.id) === beforeId) : -1;
      if (insertAt < 0) {
        const peers = room.slots.map((slot, index) => ({slot,index})).filter(({slot}) => slot.group === group && (group === 'character' || (group === 'item' ? String(slot.itemFolder || '').trim() : (String(slot.extraCategory || '').trim() || '미분류')) === folder));
        insertAt = peers.length ? peers[peers.length - 1].index + 1 : room.slots.length;
      }
      room.slots.splice(insertAt, 0, moved);
      try { await saveRoom(room); renderModalIfOpen(); }
      catch (error) { notify(`목록 이동을 저장하지 못했습니다: ${error.message}`, 'error', 5500); }
    };
    for (const [wrap, group] of [[charWrap, 'character'], [itemWrap, 'item'], [extraWrap, 'extra']]) {
      const dropTarget = event => {
        if (overlay.dataset.rpcmDragGroup !== group) return null;
        return event.target.closest('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id], .rpcm-item-folder, .rpcm-extra-folder, .rpcm-unclassified-drop');
      };
      wrap.addEventListener('dragover', event => {
        const target = dropTarget(event);
        if (!target || !wrap.contains(target)) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-drop-target').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-drop-target'));
        target.classList.add('rpcm-drop-target');
      });
      wrap.addEventListener('dragleave', event => {
        if (!wrap.contains(event.relatedTarget)) wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-drop-target').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-drop-target'));
      });
      wrap.addEventListener('drop', async event => {
        const target = dropTarget(event);
        if (!target || !wrap.contains(target)) return;
        event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
        const dragId = overlay.dataset.rpcmDragId;
        delete overlay.dataset.rpcmDragId;
        delete overlay.dataset.rpcmDragGroup;
        await moveListedSlot(group, dragId, target);
      });
      // 모바일 브라우저의 HTML 드래그 미지원 환경에서는 손잡이 터치 이동을 사용합니다.
      let touchDrag = null;
      wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
        if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
        const handle = event.target.closest('.rpcm-drag-handle');
        if (!handle || !wrap.contains(handle)) return;
        touchDrag = { id:handle.closest('.rpcm-slot')?.dataset.slotId, pointerId:event.pointerId };
        handle.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
        event.preventDefault();
      });
      wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
        if (!touchDrag || event.pointerId !== touchDrag.pointerId) return;
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-drop-target').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-drop-target'));
        const hit = document.elementFromPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY)?.closest('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id], .rpcm-item-folder, .rpcm-extra-folder, .rpcm-unclassified-drop');
        if (hit && wrap.contains(hit)) hit.classList.add('rpcm-drop-target');
      });
      wrap.addEventListener('pointerup', event => {
        if (!touchDrag || event.pointerId !== touchDrag.pointerId) return;
        const id = touchDrag.id;
        touchDrag = null;
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-drop-target').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-drop-target'));
        const hit = document.elementFromPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY)?.closest('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id], .rpcm-item-folder, .rpcm-extra-folder, .rpcm-unclassified-drop');
        if (hit && wrap.contains(hit)) void moveListedSlot(group, id, hit);
      });
      wrap.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { touchDrag = null; });
      wrap.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
        const handle = event.target.closest('.rpcm-drag-handle');
        if (!handle || !['ArrowUp','ArrowDown'].includes(event.key)) return;
        const card = handle.closest('.rpcm-slot');
        const peers = [...(card.closest('.rpcm-item-folder-body, .rpcm-extra-folder-body') || wrap).querySelectorAll(':scope > .rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]')];
        const index = peers.indexOf(card);
        const destination = event.key === 'ArrowUp' ? peers[index - 1] : peers[index + 2] || card.closest('.rpcm-item-folder, .rpcm-extra-folder') || wrap.querySelector('.rpcm-unclassified-drop');
        if (!destination) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        void moveListedSlot(group, card.dataset.slotId, destination);
      });
    }

    const mobileSectionIds = ['rpcm-section-compression','rpcm-section-longterm', 'rpcm-section-preferences', 'rpcm-section-overview', 'rpcm-section-update', 'rpcm-section-basic', 'rpcm-section-timeline', 'rpcm-section-people', 'rpcm-section-character', 'rpcm-section-item', 'rpcm-section-lore', 'rpcm-section-extra', 'rpcm-section-tools', 'rpcm-section-ai'];
    const activateMobileSection = (sectionId, resetScroll = true) => {
      if (!isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
      const wanted = mobileSectionIds.includes(sectionId) ? sectionId : 'rpcm-section-overview';
      if (!overlay.querySelector(`#${wanted}`)) return;
      state.mobileSectionId = wanted;
      const mobileViews={'rpcm-section-compression':'compression','rpcm-section-longterm':'longterm','rpcm-section-preferences':'preferences','rpcm-section-overview':'overview','rpcm-section-update':'update','rpcm-section-basic':'memory','rpcm-section-timeline':'timeline','rpcm-section-people':'people','rpcm-section-character':'characters','rpcm-section-item':'items','rpcm-section-lore':'lore','rpcm-section-extra':'extra','rpcm-section-tools':'tools','rpcm-section-ai':'ai'};
      const shell=overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-workspace');if(shell)showWorkView(overlay,mobileViews[wanted]||'overview',false);
      mobileSectionIds.forEach(id => overlay.querySelector(`#${id}`)?.classList.toggle('rpcm-mobile-section-active', id === wanted));
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-jump').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('is-active', button.dataset.jump === wanted));
      const activeMobileView = wanted === 'rpcm-section-basic' ? (state.memoryPane === 'logs' ? 'logs' : 'memory') : (mobileViews[wanted] || 'overview');
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-mobile-header-nav [data-workview]').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('is-active', button.dataset.workview === activeMobileView));
      shell?.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-work-nav [data-topview]').forEach(button => button.classList.toggle('is-active', button.dataset.topview === shell.dataset.topGroup));
      const strip = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-mobile-nav-strip');
      const activeTab = strip?.querySelector('.rpcm-jump.is-active');
      if (strip && activeTab) {
        const stripRect = strip.getBoundingClientRect();
        const tabRect = activeTab.getBoundingClientRect();
        strip.scrollLeft += tabRect.left - stripRect.left - (stripRect.width - tabRect.width) / 2;
      }
      if (resetScroll) {
        const body = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-body');
        if (body) body.scrollTop = 0;
      }
    };

    if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection(state.mobileSectionId, false);

    if (!overlay._rpcmNavDelegated) {
      overlay._rpcmNavDelegated = true;
      overlay.addEventListener('click', event => {
        const button = event.target.closest('.rpcm-jump');
        if (!button || !overlay.contains(button)) return;
        if (isMobileManagerLayout()) {
          const mobileView = button.dataset.workview || button.dataset.arg || '';
          if (mobileView && Object.hasOwn(RPCM_WORK_VIEWS, mobileView)) showWorkView(overlay, mobileView, true);
          else activateMobileSection(button.dataset.jump, true);
          const mobileHeaderMenu = button.closest('.rpcm-header-menu');
          if (mobileHeaderMenu) mobileHeaderMenu.open = false;
        } else showWorkView(overlay, button.dataset.arg || button.dataset.workview || 'overview', true);
      });
    }
    const workNav = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-work-nav');
    if (workNav) workNav.onkeydown = event => {
      if (!['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) return;
      const buttons = [...workNav.querySelectorAll('button[data-workview]')];
      const index = buttons.indexOf(event.target.closest('button[data-workview]'));
      if (index < 0) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const next = event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? buttons.length - 1
        : (index + (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1) + buttons.length) % buttons.length;
      buttons[next].focus();
      showWorkView(overlay, buttons[next].dataset.workview, true);
    };
    bindManualUpdateCenter(overlay, room);
    longTermBind(overlay, room);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-ai-memory-open-settings]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>showWorkView(overlay,'tools',true));
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-maintenance-open')?.addEventListener('click', () => openMaintenanceDialog(room));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-overview-maintenance]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const entry = maintenanceExtraImmediate[Number(button.dataset.overviewMaintenance)];
      if (entry) openMaintenanceTarget(overlay, entry);
    }));
    const openUpdate = overlay.querySelector('[data-open-update]');
    if (openUpdate) openUpdate.onclick = () => isMobileManagerLayout() ? activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-update', true) : showWorkView(overlay, 'update', true);
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-overview-issue]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
      const issue = button.dataset.overviewIssue;
      if (issue === 'logs') { overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-resolve-duplicate-logs')?.click(); return; }
      if (issue === 'items') { overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-item-review-duplicates')?.click(); return; }
      if (issue === 'lore') {
        if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-lore', true);
        else showWorkView(overlay, 'lore', true);
        overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-lore .rpcm-lore-status')?.scrollIntoView({ block:'start' });
        return;
      }
      if (issue === 'timeline') {
        if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-timeline', true);
        else showWorkView(overlay, 'timeline', true);
        overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-timeline .rpcm-story-launchbar')?.scrollIntoView({ block:'start' });
        return;
      }
      if (issue === 'knowledge') {
        if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-people', true);
        else showWorkView(overlay, 'people', true);
        const advanced = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-person-advanced');
        state.personAdvancedOpen = true;
        if (advanced) { advanced.hidden = false; advanced.open = true; }
        state.personPane = 'knowledge'; state.personFilter = '';
        renderPersonPanel(room, overlay);
        const conflict = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-card.has-conflict');
        if (conflict) { conflict.open = true; conflict.scrollIntoView({ block:'center' }); conflict.querySelector('summary')?.focus({ preventScroll:true }); }
      }
    });

    const quickbar = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-quickbar');
    const headerMenu = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-header-menu');
    const headerSearchToggle = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-search-toggle');
    const headerSearchPop = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-search-pop');
    const setHeaderSearchOpen = open => {
      if (open && headerMenu) headerMenu.open = false;
      headerSearchPop.hidden = !open;
      headerSearchToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
      headerSearchToggle.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? '통합 검색 닫기' : '통합 검색 열기');
      headerSearchToggle.innerHTML = rpcmUiIcon(open ? 'close' : 'search');
      if (open) setTimeout(() => {
        const input = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-input');
        try { input?.focus({ preventScroll:true }); } catch (_) { input?.focus(); }
      }, 0);
    };
    headerSearchToggle.onclick = () => setHeaderSearchOpen(headerSearchPop.hidden);
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-user-memo-open').onclick = () => openUserCanonMemoDialog(room);
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-notice-open').onclick = () => openNoticeCenter(room);
    const noticePreferences=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-notification-preferences');
    noticePreferences?.querySelector('[data-pref-notice-open]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>openNoticeCenter(room));
    noticePreferences?.addEventListener('change',event=>{
      const target=event.target,center=noticeState(room),setting=target.dataset.prefNotice;
      if(setting==='enabled')center.enabled=target.checked;
      else if(setting==='updateTurns')center.updateTurns=Math.max(5,Math.min(1000,Number(target.value)||30));
      else if(setting==='longTermLimit')center.longTermLimit=Math.max(5,Math.min(200,Number(target.value)||30));
      else if(target.dataset.prefNoticeCategory)center.categories[target.dataset.prefNoticeCategory]=target.checked;
      else if(target.dataset.prefNoticeArea)center.areaEnabled[target.dataset.prefNoticeArea]=target.checked;
      else if(target.dataset.prefNoticeTurns)center.areaTurns[target.dataset.prefNoticeTurns]=Math.max(5,Math.min(1000,Number(target.value)||center.updateTurns));
      else return;
      queueRoomAutoSave(room,400);noticeReconcile(room);updateNoticeBadge(room);
    });
    const quickToggle = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-header-quick-toggle');
    if (quickToggle) quickToggle.onclick = () => openQuickInjectionPanel().catch(error => notify(error.message, 'error'));

    const exitMobileEditing = () => {
      overlay.classList.remove('rpcm-mobile-editing');
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-mobile-active-slot,.rpcm-mobile-active-edit-section,.rpcm-mobile-active-editor,.rpcm-mobile-active-guide').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-mobile-active-slot','rpcm-mobile-active-edit-section','rpcm-mobile-active-editor','rpcm-mobile-active-guide'));
      updateViewportMetrics();
    };
    overlay.onfocusin = event => {
      const textarea = event.target.closest?.('textarea[data-rpcm-editor="true"]');
      if (!textarea || !isMobileManagerLayout()) return;
      const slotCard = textarea.closest('.rpcm-slot');
      const section = textarea.closest('.rpcm-section,.rpcm-tools');
      if (!slotCard || !section) return;
      activateMobileSection(section.id, false);
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-mobile-active-slot,.rpcm-mobile-active-edit-section,.rpcm-mobile-active-editor,.rpcm-mobile-active-guide').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('rpcm-mobile-active-slot','rpcm-mobile-active-edit-section','rpcm-mobile-active-editor','rpcm-mobile-active-guide'));
      slotCard.classList.add('rpcm-mobile-active-slot');
      section.classList.add('rpcm-mobile-active-edit-section');
      textarea.classList.add('rpcm-mobile-active-editor');
      textarea.closest('.rpcm-guide-panel')?.classList.add('rpcm-mobile-active-guide');
      overlay.classList.add('rpcm-mobile-editing');
      const title = slotCard.querySelector('.rpcm-slot-name')?.textContent?.trim() || '내용';
      const titleEl = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-mobile-edit-title');
      const countEl = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-mobile-edit-count');
      if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = `${title}${textarea.classList.contains('rpcm-guide-textarea') ? ' 지침' : ''} 편집`;
      if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `${formatCount(textarea.value.length)}자`;
      updateViewportMetrics();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { textarea.scrollIntoView({ block:'nearest' }); });
    };
    overlay.onfocusout = () => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const active = document.activeElement;
        if (!overlay.classList.contains('rpcm-mobile-editing') || active?.matches?.('textarea[data-rpcm-editor="true"]')) return;
        exitMobileEditing();
      }, 80);
    };
    const mobileEditDone = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-mobile-edit-done');
    if (mobileEditDone) mobileEditDone.onclick = () => {
      const active = document.activeElement;
      if (active instanceof HTMLElement) active.blur();
      exitMobileEditing();
    };

    const searchInput = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-input');
    const searchCount = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-count');
    const searchResults = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-results');
    let searchMatches = [];
    let searchItems = [];
    let searchIndex = -1;

    const searchSnippet = (value, start, end, radius = 54) => {
      const src = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      if (!src) return '';
      // raw 기준 위치와 공백 정리 후 위치는 정확히 같지 않을 수 있으므로,
      // 검색어 주변을 보여줄 때는 별도로 원문 슬라이스를 먼저 만든 뒤 공백만 정리합니다.
      const raw = String(value || '');
      const from = Math.max(0, Number(start || 0) - radius);
      const to = Math.min(raw.length, Number(end || start || 0) + radius);
      let snippet = raw.slice(from, to).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      if (from > 0) snippet = `…${snippet}`;
      if (to < raw.length) snippet = `${snippet}…`;
      return snippet || src.slice(0, radius * 2);
    };

    const renderSearchResults = () => {
      if (!searchResults) return;
      const query = String(searchInput?.value || '').trim();
      if (!query) {
        searchResults.hidden = true;
        searchResults.innerHTML = '';
        return;
      }
      if (!searchItems.length) {
        searchResults.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-search-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
        searchResults.hidden = false;
        return;
      }
      const visible = [];
      const shownByStore = new Map();
      for (const item of searchItems) {
        const store = item.store || item.kind;
        const shown = shownByStore.get(store) || 0;
        if (shown >= 8) continue;
        shownByStore.set(store, shown + 1);
        visible.push(item);
        if (visible.length >= 72) break;
      }
      searchResults.innerHTML = visible.map((item, index) => `<button type="button" class="rpcm-search-result" data-search-item="${index}"><span class="rpcm-search-result-head"><span class="rpcm-search-result-kind">${esc(item.kind)}</span><strong>${esc(item.title)}</strong></span><span class="rpcm-search-result-count">${item.count > 1 ? `${item.count}곳 · ` : ''}이동</span><span class="rpcm-search-result-store">${esc(item.store || item.kind)}</span><span class="rpcm-search-result-snippet">${esc(item.snippet)}</span></button>`).join('') + (searchItems.length > visible.length ? `<div class="rpcm-search-result-more">저장소별 최대 8개 표시 · 전체 일치 ${searchMatches.length}곳</div>` : '');
      searchResults.hidden = false;
      searchResults.querySelectorAll('[data-search-item]').forEach(button => {
        button.onclick = () => {
          const item = visible[Number(button.dataset.searchItem)];
          if (!item) return;
          showSearchMatchAt(item.matchIndex, true);
        };
      });
    };

    const collectSearchMatches = () => {
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const queryRaw = String(searchInput?.value || '').trim();
      const query = queryRaw.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
      const maxSlotSearchMatches = 1200;
      searchMatches = [];
      searchItems = [];
      searchIndex = -1;
      if (searchInput) delete searchInput.dataset.searchIndex;
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot.is-search-hit').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('is-search-hit'));
      if (!query) {
        if (searchCount) searchCount.textContent = '0 / 0';
        renderSearchResults();
        return;
      }

      const addSearchSource = (slot, sourceText, baseOffset, title, kind) => {
        const raw = String(sourceText || '');
        const haystack = raw.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
        if (!raw || !haystack.includes(query)) return;
        const firstMatchIndex = searchMatches.length;
        let from = 0;
        let firstAt = -1;
        let localCount = 0;
        while (from <= haystack.length) {
          const at = haystack.indexOf(query, from);
          if (at < 0) break;
          if (firstAt < 0) firstAt = at;
          searchMatches.push({
            slotId:String(slot.id),
            start:Number(baseOffset || 0) + at,
            end:Number(baseOffset || 0) + at + queryRaw.length,
          });
          localCount++;
          from = at + Math.max(1, query.length);
          if (searchMatches.length >= maxSlotSearchMatches) break;
        }
        if (localCount) {
          searchItems.push({
            slotId:String(slot.id),
            title:String(title || slot.title || slot.id || '검색 결과'),
            kind:String(kind || (slot.group === 'character' ? '캐릭터' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : slot.group === 'extra' ? '기타' : slot.id === 'currentState' ? '현재상태' : '메모')),
            store:slot.id === 'currentState' ? '기억 관리 › 현재상태' : slot.id === 'logSummary' ? '기억 관리 › 날짜로그' : slot.group === 'character' ? '캐릭터 설정' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : '기타',
            count:localCount,
            matchIndex:firstMatchIndex,
            snippet:searchSnippet(raw, firstAt, firstAt + queryRaw.length),
          });
        }
      };

      for (const slot of room.slots || []) {
        const content = String(slot.content || '');
        if (slot.id === 'logSummary') {
          const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(content);
          if (blocks.length) {
            for (const block of blocks) {
              addSearchSource(slot, block.raw, block.sourceStart, block.titleText || slot.title, block.isUnknown ? '날짜 미상' : '날짜로그');
              if (searchMatches.length >= maxSlotSearchMatches) break;
            }
          } else {
            addSearchSource(slot, content, 0, slot.title || '로그요약', '로그요약');
          }
        } else {
          addSearchSource(slot, content, 0, slot.title || slot.id, slot.group === 'character' ? '캐릭터' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : slot.group === 'extra' ? '기타' : slot.id === 'currentState' ? '현재상태' : '메모');
          // 제목/별칭만 검색어가 잡히고 본문에는 없을 때도 결과 카드 하나는 보여줍니다.
          const meta = [slot.title, ...(Array.isArray(slot.aliases) ? slot.aliases : [])].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
          if (meta.includes(query) && !content.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').includes(query) && searchMatches.length < maxSlotSearchMatches) {
            const matchIndex = searchMatches.length;
            searchMatches.push({ slotId:String(slot.id), start:0, end:0 });
            searchItems.push({ slotId:String(slot.id), title:String(slot.title || slot.id), kind:slot.group === 'character' ? '캐릭터' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : slot.group === 'extra' ? '기타' : '메모', store:slot.group === 'character' ? '캐릭터 설정' : slot.group === 'item' ? '중요 물품' : '기타', count:1, matchIndex, snippet:String(content || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 110) || '제목/별칭에서 일치' });
          }
        }
        if (searchMatches.length >= maxSlotSearchMatches) break;
      }
      const addStructuredSearchSource = (type, id, title, kind, store, value, person = '', pane = '') => {
        const raw = String(value || '');
        const at = raw.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').indexOf(query);
        if (at < 0 || searchMatches.length >= 2000) return;
        const matchIndex = searchMatches.length;
        searchMatches.push({ type, id:String(id), person, pane });
        searchItems.push({ title:String(title || '제목 없음'), kind, store, count:1, matchIndex, snippet:searchSnippet(raw, at, at + queryRaw.length) });
      };
      for (const card of normalizeKnowledgeCards(room).filter(item => !item.archived)) {
        addStructuredSearchSource('knowledge', card.id, `${card.person || '인물'} · ${card.fact || '인지 카드'}`, '인지', '인물정보 › 인지', [card.person,card.fact,card.actualFact,card.evidence].join('\n'), String(card.person || '').trim(), 'knowledge');
      }
      for (const record of normalizePersonStateRecords(room).filter(item => item.active)) {
        addStructuredSearchSource('person-record', record.id, `${record.person || '인물'} → ${record.target || '상대'}`, record.type === 'speech' ? '호칭·말투' : '관계·감정선', `인물정보 › ${record.type === 'speech' ? '호칭·말투' : '관계·감정선'}`, [record.person,record.target,record.address,record.tone,record.context,record.official,record.stance,record.conflict,record.evidence].join('\n'), String(record.person || '').trim(), record.type);
      }
      for (const card of storyTimelineCardsFor(room)) {
        addStructuredSearchSource('story', card.cardId, card.title || '타임라인 카드', '타임라인', `타임라인 › ${card.timelineLabel || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE}`, [card.dateStart,card.dateEnd,card.title,card.content,...(card.tags || [])].join('\n'));
      }
      for (const pack of lorePacksFor(room)) for (const card of pack.cards || []) {
        const fields = loreCardContentFields(card);
        addStructuredSearchSource('lore', card.id, card.title || '자료 카드', '자료집', `자료집 › ${pack.title || '제목 없는 팩'}`, [fields.title,fields.full,fields.compact,fields.micro,fields.exactQuote,fields.speaker,fields.target,fields.location,fields.date,...fields.aliases,...fields.triggers,...fields.entities].join('\n'));
      }
      const restoreIndex = searchInput?.dataset.restoreIndex;
      if (restoreIndex !== undefined) {
        searchIndex = searchMatches.length ? Math.max(0, Math.min(searchMatches.length - 1, Number(restoreIndex) || 0)) : -1;
        searchInput.dataset.searchIndex = searchIndex >= 0 ? String(searchIndex) : '';
        delete searchInput.dataset.restoreIndex;
      }
      if (searchCount) searchCount.textContent = searchMatches.length ? (searchIndex >= 0 ? `${searchIndex + 1} / ${searchMatches.length}` : `– / ${searchMatches.length}`) : '0 / 0';
      renderSearchResults();
    };

    const showSearchMatchAt = (index, hideDropdown = false) => {
      if (!searchMatches.length) { collectSearchMatches(); if (!searchMatches.length) return; }
      searchIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(searchMatches.length - 1, Number(index) || 0));
      if (searchInput) searchInput.dataset.searchIndex = String(searchIndex);
      const match = searchMatches[searchIndex];
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot.is-search-hit').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('is-search-hit'));
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-inline-story-card.is-search-hit,.rpcm-knowledge-card.is-search-hit,.rpcm-person-record.is-search-hit').forEach(node => node.classList.remove('is-search-hit'));
      if (match.type === 'story' || match.type === 'knowledge' || match.type === 'person-record' || match.type === 'lore') {
        let target = null;
        if (match.type === 'lore') {
          if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-lore', false);
          else showWorkView(overlay, 'lore', false);
          const pack = lorePacksFor(room).find(item => (item.cards || []).some(card => String(card.id) === match.id));
          if (pack) {
            const ui = loreUiFor(room); ui.search = ''; ui.type = ''; ui.mode = ''; ui.pack = '';ui.openPacks[pack.id]=true;ui.openCards[match.id]=true;
            const section = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-lore');
            section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-search').value = '';
            section.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-lore-filters select').forEach(select => select.value = '');
            rpcmPatchHtml(section.querySelector('#rpcm-lore-list'), renderLoreListHtml(room));
            target = [...section.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-lore-card[data-lore-card]')].find(node => node.dataset.loreCard === match.id);
            const packNode=target?.closest('.rpcm-lore-pack');if(packNode)packNode.open=true;
            if(target)target.open=true;
          }
        } else if (match.type === 'story') {
          if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-timeline', false);
          else showWorkView(overlay, 'timeline', false);
          target = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-inline-story-card[data-inline-story-id]')].find(node => node.dataset.inlineStoryId === match.id);
          const month = target?.closest('.rpcm-inline-story-month');
          if (month) month.open = true;
        } else {
          if (isMobileManagerLayout()) activateMobileSection('rpcm-section-people', false);
          else showWorkView(overlay, 'people', false);
          const advanced = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-person-advanced');
          state.personAdvancedOpen = true;
          if (advanced) { advanced.hidden = false; advanced.open = true; }
          state.personPane = match.pane;
          state.personFilter = match.person;
          state.personReturnSource = { chatId:String(room.chatId), name:match.person, id:match.id, kind:match.type === 'knowledge' ? 'knowledge' : match.pane };
          renderPersonPanel(room, overlay);
          const selector = match.type === 'knowledge' ? '.rpcm-knowledge-card[data-knowledge-id]' : '.rpcm-person-record[data-person-record]';
          target = [...overlay.querySelectorAll(selector)].find(node => (match.type === 'knowledge' ? node.dataset.knowledgeId : node.dataset.personRecord) === match.id);
          if (target) target.open = true;
        }
        setHeaderSearchOpen(false);
        if (target) { target.classList.add('is-search-hit'); target.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' }); target.querySelector('summary')?.focus({ preventScroll:true }); }
        if (searchCount) searchCount.textContent = `${searchIndex + 1} / ${searchMatches.length}`;
        if (hideDropdown && searchResults) searchResults.hidden = true;
        return;
      }
      const card = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]')].find(node => String(node.dataset.slotId) === match.slotId);
      const textarea = card?.querySelector('.rpcm-textarea');
      if (!card || !textarea) return;
      const itemFolder = card.closest('.rpcm-item-folder');
      if (itemFolder) itemFolder.open = true;
      const extraFolder = card.closest('.rpcm-extra-folder');
      if (extraFolder) extraFolder.open = true;
      const memoryPane = card.closest('.rpcm-memory-pane');
      if (memoryPane) activateMemoryPane(memoryPane.dataset.memoryContent);
      const targetSection = card.closest('.rpcm-section,.rpcm-tools');
      if (isMobileManagerLayout() && targetSection?.id) activateMobileSection(targetSection.id, false);
      else if (targetSection?.id) showWorkView(overlay, ({ 'rpcm-section-basic':'memory', 'rpcm-section-people':'people', 'rpcm-section-character':'characters', 'rpcm-section-item':'items', 'rpcm-section-extra':'extra', 'rpcm-section-tools':'tools' })[targetSection.id] || 'memory', false);
      setHeaderSearchOpen(false);
      card.open = true;
      if (card.querySelector('.rpcm-edit')?.hidden) card.querySelector('.rpcm-raw-edit-toggle,.rpcm-item-edit-toggle')?.click();
      card.classList.add('is-search-hit');
      card.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
      setTimeout(() => {
        textarea.focus();
        if (match.end > match.start) textarea.setSelectionRange(match.start, match.end);
        else textarea.setSelectionRange(0, 0);
      }, 180);
      if (searchCount) searchCount.textContent = `${searchIndex + 1} / ${searchMatches.length}`;
      if (hideDropdown && searchResults) searchResults.hidden = true;
    };

    const showSearchMatch = direction => {
      if (!searchMatches.length) { collectSearchMatches(); if (!searchMatches.length) return; }
      const next = (searchIndex + direction + searchMatches.length) % searchMatches.length;
      showSearchMatchAt(next, true);
    };

    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.oninput = collectSearchMatches;
      searchInput.onfocus = () => { if (String(searchInput.value || '').trim() && searchResults?.innerHTML) searchResults.hidden = false; };
      searchInput.onkeydown = event => {
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (searchItems.length) showSearchMatchAt(searchItems[0].matchIndex, true);
          else showSearchMatch(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
        }
        if (event.key === 'Escape' && searchResults && !searchResults.hidden) {
          event.stopPropagation();
          searchResults.hidden = true;
        }
      };
    }
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-prev').onclick = () => showSearchMatch(-1);
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-search-next').onclick = () => showSearchMatch(1);
    overlay.onmousedown = event => {
      if (searchResults && !event.target.closest('.rpcm-search-box')) searchResults.hidden = true;
      if (!headerSearchPop.hidden && !event.target.closest('#rpcm-header-search-pop,#rpcm-header-search-toggle')) setHeaderSearchOpen(false);
      if (headerMenu?.open && !event.target.closest('.rpcm-header-menu')) headerMenu.open = false;
    };
    overlay.onkeydown = event => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && !headerSearchPop.hidden) { event.preventDefault(); setHeaderSearchOpen(false); headerSearchToggle.focus(); return; }
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && headerMenu?.open) { event.preventDefault(); headerMenu.open = false; headerMenu.querySelector('summary')?.focus(); return; }
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && mainHelpPanel && !mainHelpPanel.hidden) {
        event.preventDefault();
        mainHelpPanel.hidden = true;
        return;
      }
      if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.shiftKey && event.key.toLocaleLowerCase() === 'f') {
        event.preventDefault(); setHeaderSearchOpen(true); searchInput?.select(); return;
      }
      if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key === 'Enter') {
        const arm = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-arm');
        if (arm && !room.pending && !arm.disabled && !document.querySelector('#rpcm-preview-backdrop,#rpcm-import-backdrop')) { event.preventDefault(); arm.click(); }
        return;
      }
    };

    const activeTimelineMain = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-active-log-timeline-main');
    if (activeTimelineMain) activeTimelineMain.onchange = async () => {
      const previous = activeLogTimelineLabel(room);
      const desired = cleanLogTimelineLabel(activeTimelineMain.value) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
      room.activeLogTimeline = desired;
      normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room);
      try {
        if (room.pending && room.autoLogRecallEnabled) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'active-log-timeline-change');
        await saveRoom(room);
        notify(`다음 날짜 로그 저장 위치를 ‘${desired}’으로 바꿨습니다. API 요약과 수기 추가 로그가 이 시간선에 저장됩니다.`, 'success', 4600);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) {
        room.activeLogTimeline = previous;
        activeTimelineMain.value = previous;
        notify(`시간선 변경 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000);
      }
    };

    const autoLogCb = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-log');
    const autoLogRelevance = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-auto-log-relevance');
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-log-count-quick')?.addEventListener('click', () => showWorkView(overlay,'logs',true));
    overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-context-refresh')?.addEventListener('click', async event => {
      const button = event.currentTarget;
      const original = button.textContent;
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = '최신 RP 읽는 중…';
      try {
        const result = await refreshInjectionSelectionFromLatestRp(room);
        const sync = result.slotSync || {added:0,removed:0,updated:0};
        notify(`주입 재검사 완료 · 캐릭터 ${result.characters} · 중요 물품 ${result.items} · 인물정보 ${result.people}${result.pending ? ` · 현재 주입 정리 +${sync.added}/−${sync.removed}/갱신 ${sync.updated}` : ' · 다음 주입 후보 갱신'}`, 'success', 5600);
        renderModalIfOpen();
        state.modal?.querySelector('[data-injection-recheck-report]')?.setAttribute('open','');
      } catch (error) {
        notify(`주입 내용 새로고침 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6500);
        if (button.isConnected) { button.disabled = false; button.textContent = original; }
      }
    });
    for (const [id,field,defaultCount] of [['rpcm-log-recent-count','autoLogRecentBlocks',2],['rpcm-log-related-count','autoLogRelatedBlocks',APP.defaultRelatedLogBlocks]]) {
      const select=overlay.querySelector('#'+id);
      if (!select) continue;
      select.onchange=async()=>{
        const old=room[field];
        const next=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Number(select.value)||0));
        if(room.pending && room.autoLogRecallEnabled && !confirm(`현재 RP 주입 중입니다. 날짜로그 ${field==='autoLogRecentBlocks'?'최근':'관련'} 자동 개수를 ${next}개로 바꾸어 주입문에 반영할까요?\n직접 주입하거나 고정한 날짜로그는 그대로 유지됩니다.`)){
          select.value=String(old??defaultCount);return;
        }
        room[field]=next;
        try{
          if(room.pending && room.autoLogRecallEnabled) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room,'log-count-change');
          await saveRoom(room);
          notify(`날짜로그 자동 선택: 최근 ${recentLogInjectionCount(room)}개 · 관련 최대 ${relatedLogInjectionCount(room)}개`, 'success',3500);
          renderModalIfOpen();
        }catch(error){room[field]=old;select.value=String(old??defaultCount);notify(`날짜로그 개수 저장/반영 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error',6500);}
      };
    }
    if (autoLogCb) autoLogCb.onchange = async () => {
      const previous = !!room.autoLogRecallEnabled;
      const desired = autoLogCb.checked;
      if (room.pending && !confirm(`현재 컨텍스트가 주입 중입니다.\n최근·관련 로그 자동 선택을 ${desired ? '켜기' : '끄기'}로 즉시 바꿀까요?
자동 선택을 꺼도 직접 주입/📌항상 주입 날짜는 유지됩니다.`)) { autoLogCb.checked = previous; return; }
      room.autoLogRecallEnabled = desired;
      try { if (room.pending) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'log-auto-toggle'); await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (e) { room.autoLogRecallEnabled = previous; autoLogCb.checked = previous; notify(`로그 자동 선택 변경 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500); }
      renderModalIfOpen();
    };
    if (autoLogRelevance) autoLogRelevance.onchange = async () => {
      const previous = logRelevanceSetting(room);
      room.autoLogRelevance = autoLogRelevance.value;
      try { if (room.pending && room.autoLogRecallEnabled) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'log-relevance-change'); await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (e) { room.autoLogRelevance = previous; autoLogRelevance.value = previous; notify(`관련도 기준 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
      refreshStatsOnly();
    };
    const logDateFixBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-log-date-fix');
    if (logDateFixBtn) logDateFixBtn.onclick = async () => {
      readModalIntoRoom();
      const changed = await openLogDateNormalizerDialog(room);
      if (!changed) return;
      try {
        if (room.pending) await rebuildPendingLogItems(room, 'log-date-normalize');
        await saveRoom(room);
        notify('날짜/연도 정리를 로그요약에 반영했습니다.', 'success', 4200);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) { notify(`날짜 수정 반영 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
    };

    const storyInjectionCb = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-story-injection-enabled');
    if (storyInjectionCb) storyInjectionCb.onchange = async () => {
      const previous = room.storyTimelineInjectionEnabled !== false;
      const desired = !!storyInjectionCb.checked;
      if (room.pending && !confirm(`현재 컨텍스트가 주입 중입니다.\n타임라인 전체 주입을 ${desired ? '켜기' : '끄기'}로 즉시 바꿀까요?\n카드별 ‘GPT 주입 포함’ 설정은 그대로 유지됩니다.`)) {
        storyInjectionCb.checked = previous;
        return;
      }
      const previousRemoved = room.pending ? quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).map(item => ({ ...item })) : null;
      room.storyTimelineInjectionEnabled = desired;
      try {
        // 전체 ON은 사용자의 명시적인 재활성화이므로, 이 주입 세션에서 간편 관리로 뺀 타임라인도 다시 켭니다.
        if (desired && room.pending) room.pending.quickRemovedItems = quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).filter(item => !isStoryTimelinePendingItem(item));
        const result = room.pending ? await rebuildPendingStoryTimelineItem(room, 'story-timeline-master-toggle') : null;
        await saveRoom(room);
        if (desired) notify(room.pending && result?.synced ? '타임라인 주입을 켜고 현재 carrier에 즉시 반영했습니다.' : '타임라인 주입을 켰습니다. 다음 주입부터 포함됩니다.', 'success', 4300);
        else notify(result?.cleared ? '타임라인 주입을 끄고, 남은 주입 항목이 없어 현재 주입도 정상 종료했습니다.' : '타임라인 전체 주입을 껐습니다. 카드별 포함 설정은 보존됩니다.', 'success', 4500);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) {
        room.storyTimelineInjectionEnabled = previous;
        if (room.pending && previousRemoved) room.pending.quickRemovedItems = previousRemoved;
        storyInjectionCb.checked = previous;
        notify(`타임라인 주입 설정 변경 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      }
    };

    const storyManageBtn = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-story-manage');
    const runStoryDialog = async (options={}) => {
      // 타임라인 창은 다른 슬롯의 화면값을 일괄 수집하지 않고 자체 데이터만 편집합니다.
      // 주입 중이라도 저장 후 타임라인 항목만 현재 carrier에 재동기화합니다.
      const changed = await openStoryTimelineManagerDialogV35(room, options);
      if (!changed) return;

      // 먼저 타임라인 원본을 저장합니다. carrier 갱신이 실패해도 사용자의 편집본은 잃지 않습니다.
      try {
        await saveRoom(room);
      } catch (e) {
        notify(`연속성 타임라인 저장 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6500);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const syncResult = room.pending ? await rebuildPendingStoryTimelineItem(room, 'story-timeline-edit') : null;

        if (!room.pending && syncResult?.cleared) {
          notify('연속성 타임라인을 저장했습니다. 현재 주입에서 남은 항목이 없어 자동 주입은 정상 종료되었습니다.', 'success', 5200);
        } else if (syncResult?.suppressed) {
          notify(syncResult.payloadChanged
            ? '연속성 타임라인을 저장했습니다. 현재 주입에서 타임라인을 꺼둔 상태는 유지하고, 다시 켤 때 사용할 내용만 최신화했습니다.'
            : '연속성 타임라인 변경사항을 저장했습니다. 현재 주입에서 타임라인을 꺼둔 상태는 그대로 유지됩니다.', 'success', 5600);
        } else if (syncResult?.synced) {
          notify('연속성 타임라인을 저장하고 현재 주입에도 즉시 반영했습니다.', 'success', 4700);
        } else if (room.pending) {
          notify('연속성 타임라인 변경사항을 저장했습니다. 현재 주입에 들어간 타임라인 내용은 바뀌지 않았습니다.', 'success', 4700);
        } else {
          notify(room.storyTimelineInjectionEnabled === false
            ? '연속성 타임라인을 저장했습니다. 전체 주입은 OFF이며 카드별 포함 설정은 보존됩니다.'
            : '연속성 타임라인을 저장했습니다. 주입 포함 카드는 다음 주입부터 항상 들어갑니다.', 'success', 4700);
        }
        renderModalIfOpen();
      } catch (e) {
        // carrier 갱신이 실패하면 서버 carrier는 직전 검증본으로 롤백되고, 위에서 저장한 타임라인 원본은 유지됩니다.
        notify(`타임라인은 저장했지만 현재 주입 반영에 실패했습니다. carrier는 직전 검증 상태를 유지합니다: ${e.message}`, 'error', 7500);
        renderModalIfOpen();
      }
    };
    const storyDirectReplace=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-story-direct-replace');
    if(storyDirectReplace)storyDirectReplace.onclick=()=>{readModalIntoRoom();rpcmDirectPasteDialog('타임라인 · 전체 원문 붙여넣기','결과 가져오기가 실패했을 때 쓰는 복구용 전체 교체입니다. 현재 활성 시간선만 바꾸고 수동 업데이트 체크포인트는 이동하지 않습니다.','[TIMELINE]\n[CARD]\nREF=C001\nDATE_START=...\nTITLE=...\nCONTENT=\n...\n[/CARD]\n[/TIMELINE]',()=>{const run=createStoryTimelineRun(room,activeLogTimelineLabel(room),'direct-view','FULL');return serializeStoryTimelineRefMaster(snapshotStoryTimelineCardsForLabel(room,run.timelineLabel),run,true);},value=>prepareDirectTimelineReplace(room,value),prepared=>applyDirectTimelineReplace(room,prepared));};

    const timelineSection=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-timeline');
    const commitStoryCardChange=async(label,mutate)=>{
      backupStoryTimeline(room,label+' 전');
      const master=normalizeStoryTimelineCards(room);
      mutate(master);
      normalizeStoryTimelineCards(room);
      await saveRoom(room);
      if(room.pending) await rebuildPendingStoryTimelineItem(room,'timeline-inline-edit');
      renderModalIfOpen();
    };
    timelineSection?.addEventListener('click',async event=>{
      const pop=timelineSection.querySelector('.rpcm-story-popover');
      if(pop && !event.target.closest('.rpcm-story-popover,.rpcm-story-horizontal .rpcm-inline-story-top'))pop.remove();
      const horizontalSummary=event.target.closest('.rpcm-story-horizontal .rpcm-inline-story-top');
      if(horizontalSummary){event.preventDefault();const card=horizontalSummary.closest('[data-inline-story-id]');if(pop?.dataset.inlineStoryId===card?.dataset.inlineStoryId){pop.remove();return;}openInlineStoryPopover(timelineSection,card);return;}
      const viewButton=event.target.closest('[data-story-view]');
      if(viewButton){switchInlineTimelineView(timelineSection,room,viewButton.dataset.storyView);return;}
      const step=event.target.closest('[data-story-step]');
      if(step){const rail=timelineSection.querySelector('.rpcm-story-horizontal');if(rail){const card=rail.querySelector('.rpcm-inline-story-card');rail.scrollBy({left:Number(step.dataset.storyStep)*(card?.getBoundingClientRect().width||280),behavior:'smooth'});}return;}
      const jump=event.target.closest('[data-inline-story-jump]');
      if(jump){const card=[...timelineSection.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-inline-story-card[data-inline-story-id]')].find(node=>node.dataset.inlineStoryId===jump.dataset.inlineStoryJump);const month=card?.closest('.rpcm-inline-story-month');if(month)month.open=true;if(card)card.open=!timelineSection.querySelector('.rpcm-story-horizontal');card?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center',inline:'center'});if(card&&timelineSection.querySelector('.rpcm-story-horizontal'))setTimeout(()=>openInlineStoryPopover(timelineSection,card),300);return;}
      const action=event.target.closest('[data-inline-story-action]');if(!action)return;
      const node=action.closest('[data-inline-story-id]');const id=node?.dataset.inlineStoryId;
      const master=room.storyTimelineCards?.find(card=>String(card.cardId)===id);if(!master)return;
      const edit=node.querySelector('.rpcm-inline-story-edit');
      if(action.dataset.inlineStoryAction==='edit'){edit.hidden=false;node.querySelector('.rpcm-inline-story-read').hidden=true;edit.querySelector('[data-field="title"]')?.focus();return;}
      if(action.dataset.inlineStoryAction==='cancel'){edit.hidden=true;node.querySelector('.rpcm-inline-story-read').hidden=false;return;}
      if(action.dataset.inlineStoryAction==='delete'){
        if(!confirm(`‘${master.title||'제목 없는 카드'}’를 삭제할까요?`))return;
        try{await commitStoryCardChange('카드 삭제',cards=>{room.storyTimelineCards=cards.filter(card=>String(card.cardId)!==id);});}
        catch(error){notify('타임라인 삭제 실패: '+error.message,'error');}return;
      }
      if(action.dataset.inlineStoryAction==='save'){
        const value=field=>edit.querySelector(`[data-field="${field}"]`)?.value||'';
        const rawStart=value('dateStart').trim(),rawEnd=value('dateEnd').trim();
        const start=normalizeStoryDate(rawStart),end=normalizeStoryDate(rawEnd);
        if((rawStart&&!start)||(rawEnd&&!end)||(end&&start&&end<start)){notify('날짜 형식을 확인해 주세요. 종료일은 시작일보다 빠를 수 없습니다.','warn');return;}
        if(!value('title').trim()){notify('카드 제목을 입력해 주세요.','warn');return;}
        const updated={timelineLabel:cleanLogTimelineLabel(value('timelineLabel'))||DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE,dateStart:start,dateEnd:end,title:value('title').trim(),tags:[...new Set(value('tags').split(/[,，#]/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean))],content:value('content').trim(),userLocked:!!edit.querySelector('[data-field="userLocked"]')?.checked,updatedAt:nowIso()};
        try{await commitStoryCardChange('카드 편집',cards=>{const current=cards.find(card=>String(card.cardId)===id);if(current)Object.assign(current,updated);});}
        catch(error){notify('타임라인 저장 실패: '+error.message,'error');}
      }
    });
    timelineSection?.addEventListener('change',async event=>{
      const setting=event.target.closest('[data-inline-story-setting]');

      try {

        readModalIntoRoom();

        const defaultName = libraryDisplayName(lastLib) || `${room.label || '캐릭터'} 설정집`;
        const entered = prompt('설정집 이름을 입력해 주세요.\n같은 이름으로 저장하면 기존 설정집을 갱신합니다.', defaultName);
        if (entered === null) return;

        if (!presetName) { notify('설정집 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }

        const nameKey = normalizedLibraryLabel(presetName);
        let existing = allLibraries.find(lib => normalizedLibraryLabel(libraryDisplayName(lib)) === nameKey) || null;
        if (!existing && lastLib && normalizedLibraryLabel(libraryDisplayName(lastLib)) === nameKey) existing = lastLib;
        const scopeId = existing?.scopeId || presetScopeIdFromName(presetName);
        const merged = mergeCharacterLibraryItems(existing?.characters || [], choice.items, choice.preserve);
        const scopeAliases = [...new Set([...(existing?.scopeAliases || []), ...getCharacterLibraryScopeCandidates(room), scopeId])];
        await saveCharacterLibrary({
          ...(existing || {}),
          scopeId,
          kind: 'preset',
          presetName,
          label: presetName,
          sourceLabel: room.label || existing?.sourceLabel || '',
          sourceLabels: [...new Set([...(existing?.sourceLabels || []), room.label].filter(Boolean))],
          scopeAliases,
          characters: merged,
          createdAt: existing?.createdAt || nowIso(),
        });
        room.lastCharacterLibraryId = scopeId;
        if (!room.autoCharacterLibraryId) room.autoCharacterLibraryId = scopeId;
        await saveRoom(room);
        notify(`설정집 ‘${presetName}’ 저장 완료 · 선택 ${choice.items.length}명 · 총 ${merged.length}명`, 'success', 5500);
      } catch (e) { notify(`설정집 저장 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
    };

      itemChecks().forEach(check => { check.checked = false; });
